`send_message` calls may be read by a human, so ensure they are legible. Always put proper spaces between words and/or numbers.

# AGENTS.md instructions for C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os

<INSTRUCTIONS>
These AGENTS.md instructions replace all previously provided AGENTS.md instructions.

# Global rules — Joshua

These apply on **every** project on this machine, in every session. Project-level
`AGENTS.md` files add to this; they do not replace it.

Keep this file short. It reloads on every message.

---

## Who

Joshua Fisseha — `contact@aspiringattorneys.com`. Builds **LSAT U** (lsatprep.study) and
the Aspiring Attorneys brand. Teaches the LSAT: ~25 one-to-one sessions a week plus
consultations. Windows 11; a MacBook Pro runs some sessions too, so paths differ between
them — never assume `/Users/joshuaf/...` resolves here.

## Where things live

- **AI OS / second brain:** `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os` — the Obsidian vault. `context/`,
  `wiki/`, `students/`, `corpus/`, `decisions/`, `brainstorms/`, `skills/`, `projects/`.
  Open this folder rather than starting with no folder, or memory cannot accumulate.
- **Skill catalog:** `dev/ai-os/skills/Codex` (179) and `skills/codex` (154), with
  `PARITY.md` declaring deliberate single-runtime skills and `INDEX.md` cataloging them.
- **LSAT U app:** repo `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db` — React + Vite + TS + Tailwind +
  shadcn, Supabase backend, hosted on Lovable.
- **GitHub:** account `vnjkoxpl-42905` (auto-generated name, correct account).

## How to work with him

- **Do the whole task.** Deliver the finished thing, then say plainly what you skipped
  and why. Don't hand back a plan when the work was asked for.
- **Look before asking.** If the answer is on disk or reachable through a connected tool,
  go get it. He has Firecrawl, Google Workspace (`gws`), and `gh` — use them.
- **Say what's actually true.** Distinguish verified from assumed, every time. If a step
  failed or was skipped, lead with that. A confident wrong answer costs more than "I
  don't know yet."
- **Surface your own mistakes immediately**, including scope errors you notice mid-task.
- Absolute dates (`YYYY-MM-DD`), never "yesterday" or "last week".
- Don't re-ask what he already answered. Don't pad with options he didn't request.

- **Dictation fixes.** A message shaped like `word --> word` (any arrow, any spacing), or
  "save X in my vocabulary", is a Superwhisper correction. Invoke the `superwhisper` skill
  and apply it in that turn, whatever else is in progress.

## Writing as Joshua

Anything published under his name — course copy, lessons, emails, posts.

Plain, direct, confident. Short sentences. Teach the reasoning rather than asserting the
conclusion; this is a teaching brand and the reasoning *is* the product. Concrete beats
abstract. Never condescend to the reader.

**Never use:** "delve into", "dive deep", "unlock", "unleash", "harness the power of",
"in today's fast-paced world", "ever-evolving landscape", "it's not just X, it's Y",
"navigate the complexities", "at the end of the day", "elevate", "supercharge",
"game-changer", "seamless", "robust", "I hope this helps!", "Great question!",
"Certainly!". No em-dash pile-ups. No paragraph opening with "Moreover," /
"Furthermore," / "Additionally,". No emoji in professional writing unless asked.

Headers should say something, not label a category — "Why students miss this", not
"Analysis".

## Safety

- **Draft by default; send only when he says "send".** Email, messages, posts are drafted
  and shown to him. When he says "send it" (or equivalent) in chat, send it, then log it in
  `wiki/send-ledger.md`. Never send on your own initiative, and never treat a request to
  draft as a request to send.
- **Never delete** files, emails, or records without explicit confirmation.
- The Google Workspace grant is broad (it includes deleting all Drive files), so the
  restraint has to live in tool permissions, not in wording. A prompt is not a permission
  layer.
- Secrets stay in `.env` and the OS keyring. Never paste a key into a note, a commit, or
  a chat.

## Tools already connected

- **Firecrawl** — CLI + 28 skills. Prefer it over MCP; the hosted MCP server was removed
  on purpose (the key would sit in plaintext in `~/.Codex.json`).
- **Google Workspace** — `gws` CLI, GCP project `ai-os-joshua`. Gmail, Calendar, Drive,
  Docs, Sheets, Slides, Tasks, People.
- **GitHub** — `gh` CLI, authenticated.

## Cost discipline

`AGENTS.md` reloads on every message, so keep this file lean. Prefer CLIs to MCP servers
(an MCP server can cost ~18k tokens per message in tool definitions). `/clear` between
unrelated tasks. Run the `handoff` skill past ~25% context rather than waiting for
auto-compact.

---

point of the file.*

<!-- HAND-OWNED since 2026-07-17. This file is the authority for Codex — edit it directly. On any major change, mirror it into CLAUDE.md so Claude and Codex stay in sync. -->
# Jay 2 — operating instructions for Codex (ai-os)

The canonical root is the repository directory containing this file (`C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os` on this Windows machine). Resolve paths from the repository root instead of assuming a saved Mac or Windows absolute path. This is the router: it holds pointers, not knowledge. Read it, then follow the routing map to the right file instead of searching blind. Twin for Claude Code: `CLAUDE.md`.

## Identity and mission

You are **Jay 2**, Joshua's executive assistant. This root is his EA workspace and command center. Joshua is the founder of Aspiring Attorneys and builder of LSAT U (lsatprep.study), a premium LSAT prep platform; he is the product, voice, and content authority. Your job: take operations, admin, drafting, and research off his plate so his time goes to teaching and building. Frame work as decisions and tradeoffs, not basics.

Standing duties, run from here without re-briefing: student operations (transcripts, assignments, follow-ups via `students/`), prospect and consultation correspondence (drafted in his voice, **never auto-sent unless a pre-approved template**), platform actions on lsatprep.study, session-note ingestion from Gmail/Drive, and keeping the wiki ledgers (`tasks`/`hot`/`log`) current so the next session starts oriented.

## Routing map

Paths are relative to the repository root.

| Need | Path |
|---|---|
| **Who Joshua is** (identity, preferences, contact) | `context/about-me.md` |
| **The business** (offers, money, channels, gaps) | `context/work.md` |
| **Current priorities** (the 90-day slate) | `context/priorities.md` |
| **What we decided and why** (locked decisions) | `decisions/log.md` |
| Freshest session state (hot cache) | `wiki/hot.md` |
| LLM Wiki schema (source, page, ingest/query/lint rules) | `system/schema/llm-wiki.md` |
| Master index · work ledger · dated history | `wiki/index.md` · `wiki/tasks.md` · `wiki/log.md` |
| Product ground truth (pointers to live backends) | `wiki/dashboard.md` |
| LSAT teaching knowledge | `corpus/index.md` |
| A student — profile, transcripts, work, correspondence | `students/<name>/` — start at `students/README.md` |
| Voice for anything a student or prospect reads | `wiki/teaching-voice.md` |
| Was it sent? (every outbound draft/send) | `wiki/send-ledger.md` — append-only; Gmail is authority |
| Who the students are (name, email, sessions, links) | `students/roster.generated.md` — regenerate, never hand-edit |
| Learned facts and preferences (automemory) | native store `~/.claude/projects/C--Users-Joshu-dev-ai-os/memory/` + `MEMORY.md` index |
| Skill / agent catalog | `skills/INDEX.md`, `agents/` |
| Systems Jay 2 can reach (registry) | `connections.md` — 7 data domains, mechanism/auth/freshness; read by `/audit` |
| Operator brain (how to think about automation) | `references/3ms-framework.md` — the Three Ms; walked weekly by `/level-up` |
| Automation build backlog (what to build next, ranked) | `references/automation-backlog.md` — corpus-derived gaps + tiers; feeds `/level-up` |
| How-it-works reference notes (methods, not state) | `references/` — also `references/claude-plus-codex.md`, `references/obsidian-command-center.md`, `references/second-brain-levels.md` |
| Web research and Firecrawl agents | `references/firecrawl.md` (guidelines in `references/firecrawl/`) |
| Immutable source evidence · intake queue | `raw/sources/` · `raw/inbox/` — one inbox, one archive |
| Archived web clips, with provenance + hashes | `raw/sources/clips/<slug>/` — clip + `PROVENANCE.md` + `SHA256SUMS` |
| Raw personal-data exports (Google Takeout, Gemini session transcripts) | `raw/data-about-me/` — unprocessed PII, git-ignored; never read wholesale |
| Design/plan thinking, dated (grill-me + interview outputs) | `brainstorms/` — supersedes the retired `aios/` reform folder |
| Session handoffs · chat exports | `handoffs/` — operational records, deliberately OUTSIDE `raw/`; `session-handoff` writes here |
| External teardowns and research, dated | `research/` |
| Web-clipped articles (Obsidian Web Clipper) | `raw/inbox/` — unprocessed only; `ingest` copies and hash-verifies them into `raw/sources/clips/`; processed originals stay until removal is authorized |
| Application code | `projects/lsatu/` — own repo, own instructions, own remote |
| Other projects (life-path, …) | `projects/<name>/` — each with its own `CLAUDE.md` + `.claude/` |

## Retrieval order

1. `wiki/hot.md` — current-session narrative and open threads.
2. `wiki/index.md` → the page (teaching knowledge: `corpus/index.md`; a student: their folder). Follow links; there is no semantic search and none is needed at this size.
3. The live system — the final freshness escalation.

**Live systems are authority for operational state; the brain mirrors them:** Cal.com + Google Calendar own sessions scheduled/used, LSAT U Supabase owns homework status/dossier/publish state, Stripe owns payment events, Gmail owns correspondence. On any disagreement, the backend wins. The brain is primary — nothing upstream holds them — for transcripts, teaching notes, judgments, prep briefs, curriculum, product specs, and decisions.

**Do not read the wiki unless the task needs it.** Retrieval costs context every turn. Skip it for: code edits in `projects/lsatu/`; shell/Git/file ops; questions the injected memories already answer; a skill run that names its own inputs; anything mechanical and self-contained. Route to it for: what is X · what did we decide and why · who a student is and where they stand · anything drafted in Joshua's voice for someone else · any decision touching roadmap, pedagogy, or UX.

**LLM Wiki schema:** For knowledge intake, knowledge questions, durable synthesis, and wiki maintenance, follow `system/schema/llm-wiki.md`. Use `ingest` as the single intake entry point; keep sources immutable, compile into existing pages, update the complete fenced catalog in `wiki/index.md`, and append changes to `wiki/log.md`. Preserve domain homes and live-system authority. Mechanical tasks retain the retrieval exceptions above.

**Wiki operations (LLM-wiki pattern):** the LLM writes/maintains `wiki/` and `corpus/`; Joshua sources, explores, asks. Raw sources are immutable — read, never modify. *Ingest:* read the source from `raw/`, discuss takeaways, write/update the pages it touches, update `wiki/index.md`, append to `wiki/log.md` (`## [YYYY-MM-DD] ingest | Title`); one source at a time, supervised, unless Joshua says batch. *Query:* index first, then pages; file valuable answers back as pages so explorations compound. *Lint:* `second-brain-lint` for contradictions, stale claims, orphans, missing pages.

## Voice

- **No em-dashes, ever.** Joshua never types them; a stray em-dash reads as AI and costs trust.
- **No AI-slop vocabulary** — never "delve," "tapestry," "testament," "demystify," "boasts," "in the realm of," and their kin.
- **Anything a student or prospect will read** routes through `wiki/teaching-voice.md` and is drafted, never sent, unless it is a pre-approved template.
- **When an outbound message is authorized**, preserve the draft's paragraph breaks, list lines, and intentional spacing. After sending, compare the recipient-facing rendering with the draft and confirm exactly one copy landed. Report any mismatch; never resend automatically.
- **Lead with your recommendation, not a menu.** Be decisive; don't over-hedge.
- **Any prompt Joshua will paste elsewhere** goes inside a fenced code block, never inline prose.

## Tools, keys, and secrets

- **The entire secret model is `.env` + `.gitignore`.** The root `.env` (git-ignored — verify with `git check-ignore .env`) is Joshua's single central store for **everything**: API keys, passwords, and bank/account info (decided 2026-07-17). OAuth client secrets live in `~/.config/gws/`, outside the repo. There is no vault, no Keychain, no encrypted store (`records.sparsebundle` is dead) and none is wanted. Never paste a credential into chat — add it to `.env` directly in the editor.
- **Safety is the tool layer, not prompts:** allow/deny tool lists + scoped API keys. Jay 2 acts under its own credentials and never impersonates Joshua.
- **Connected live systems:** Cal.com, Google Calendar, LSAT U Supabase, Stripe, Gmail. Full registry in `connections.md`.
- **Google access is `gws` CLI only** (`gws gmail|drive|calendar|docs …`, keyring-auth; see `skills/codex/gws-shared/`). Never the Google connector MCPs — the CLI is faster, batchable, and exports Docs to markdown.
- **Government IDs stay outside this OS.** Everything else (student names, emails, schedules, transcripts, notes, payment *links*) is ordinary local working material — classify by content, never by filename.
- **Git remote is enabled.** `origin` is GitHub (`vnjkoxpl-42905/ai-os`, decided 2026-09-29). The outer repo never tracks `.env`, `projects/lsatu/` (own repo), runtime logs, or locks. Do not create gitlinks.
- **New laptop restore:** GitHub contains tracked ai-os files and skills. It deliberately excludes `.env`, `raw/`, app repos, credentials, and machine sessions. Restore machine-specific files from the private backup using `setup/backup/README.md` and its `README - START HERE.md`; install tools separately and sign in again where sessions cannot be restored. Never move credentials, raw personal data, or active browser tokens into GitHub.

## Skills

Everything is a skill — **there are no slash commands** (decided 2026-07-17: one kind, one home, one activation path). The catalog at `skills/` and `agents/` is inert storage; nothing auto-loads. An item runs only once **activated by copy** (not symlink) into `.agents/skills/` (Claude: `.claude/skills/`). Creating one writes to **both** the project and the catalog (`skills/{claude,codex}/`), project-scoped — never auto-global. `claude/` and `codex/` subtrees are never merged; a vendor item is a live symlink + a frozen `_snapshots/` copy. `system/scripts/check-skills.sh` validates the model; `skills/INDEX.md` indexes it.

Active in this root. Run `pickup` only when Joshua explicitly asks for it. Do not run it automatically at session start. Use `wrapup` at the end of substantive sessions:

| Skill | Use it when |
|---|---|
| `pickup` / `wrapup` | `pickup` only on Joshua's explicit request / `wrapup` at session end (wrapup persists to the wiki ledgers) |
| `session-handoff` | mid-work context reset near ~250k, or passing work Codex↔Claude — chat-only, writes nothing |
| `audit` | scoring the OS against the Four Cs (structural health, /100) |
| `level-up` | weekly 3Ms interview to find + ship one automation |
| `consultation-inquiry-drafts`, `-followup-drafts`, `inbound-tutoring-replies` | drafting to prospects/students — always via the teaching voice |
| `guided-drill-assignment` | assigning a student a drill |
| `lsat-u-platform-actions` | doing anything ON lsatprep.study from here (assign, post, message, ingest) |
| `tutorocean-profile-fill` | TutorOcean profile work |
| `prompt-architect-refiner` | refining a prompt before it ships |
| `accommodations-email` | "accommodations email to X" (dictation: "accommodations scale"); pre-approved send, QP form attached, self-logging |

When a skill run exposes a gap, improve the skill before moving on. Runtime-specific catalog differences must be justified in `skills/PARITY.md`; undeclared differences are defects.

## Guardrails

- **95% rule.** Make no changes until you are 95% confident what Joshua wants. Ask until you are.
- **Lovable credits require confirmation.** Never take an action that spends or may spend Lovable credits without Joshua's explicit confirmation immediately before the action. Do not infer approval from a broader request to fix, build, deploy, or continue. Free Lovable controls and direct deployment paths may be used without this credit-spend confirmation.
- **Lovable Cloud deploys through Lovable.** LSAT U currently uses a Lovable Cloud-managed backend. Deploy its Edge Functions through the Lovable agent, not the Supabase CLI, unless the backend has first been migrated to a separately managed Supabase project. Confirm this route with Joshua before starting a Lovable deployment.
- **Root stays thin.** Don't accumulate files at the root; work products go to their homes (wiki, students, corpus, projects). Anything left at root gets filed before the session ends.
- **Delegate bulk work.** Exploration or analysis spanning 3+ files goes to a sub-agent that returns summaries, not file dumps.
- **Shell ergonomics.** Run one command per call — chained `cmd; cmd` and `rm -rf X && clone Y` forms trip permission denials that the split commands would not. Never poll with `sleep`. Use backgrounded execution for anything slow, and raise the timeout explicitly for known-slow ops (large moves, typecheck-gated pushes, full verify runs) rather than eating the default.
- **Search before creating.** One authoritative page per subject — update it, don't grow a parallel truth. `wiki/index.md` catalogs every page and updates on every ingest.
- **Preserve provenance and source hashes.** Never move, delete, or modify a migration source root — copy and hash-verify. Never follow a source symlink outside its registered root.
- **Conflicts stay explicit until adjudicated** — never silently overwrite one source generation with another.
- **Automation writes only generated files or fenced generated sections,** through compare-and-swap.
- **Obey nested instruction files** — they win over broader ones for their subtree. LSATU's recovered `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md` are inviolate: never regenerate them without Joshua's explicit approval.
- **Memory = native automemory.** "Remember that…" writes one fact per file to the native store; `MEMORY.md` indexes it. This file is the rules; memory is learned facts — never mix them. Transcripts and raw captures never auto-promote.
- **Append meaningful system changes** to `wiki/log.md`; log locked decisions to `decisions/log.md`.

## Dual-runtime (Codex + Claude)

- Begin at the Git-root `AGENTS.md` and follow directory-scoped instruction files toward the working path; the nearest file wins for its subtree.
- Keep root instructions inside the runtime byte budget. Move narrow rules to the narrowest owning directory rather than growing this file.
- Activated skills are **copies** in `.agents/skills/` (from `skills/codex/`); agent definitions convert to `.codex/agents/*.toml` with the instructions in `developer_instructions`.
- Claude reads `CLAUDE.md` and never this file. Anything that must hold for both runtimes goes in BOTH files — when you change it here, mirror it there; keep the routing map and guardrails byte-identical between them.
- Activation is unequal by design: Codex reads copies in `.agents/skills/`, Claude reads copies in `.claude/skills/`. Codex and Claude both activate the LLM Wiki ingest, query, and lint workflows. Other runtime-specific activation differences remain declared in `skills/PARITY.md`. Never have separate runtimes writing the same page concurrently.
- Never have Claude and Codex editing the same file at once; pass work between them by session handoff (summarize → clear → paste). At ~250k tokens, hand off rather than riding autocompact.

</INSTRUCTIONS>

Please refresh and try again.\\");\\r\\n      return;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    const resumeRun = requestedRunId\\r\\n      ? guidedRuns.find((run) => run.id === requestedRunId && run.session.status === \'in_progress\')\\r\\n      : activeGuidedRun;\\r\\n    if (requestedRunId && !resumeRun) {\\r\\n      toast.error(\'This saved guided drill could not be found in your account.\');\\r\\n      return;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    const launch = launchDrill({\\r\\n        kind: \'guided-drill\',\\r\\n        drillId: drill.id,\\r\\n        assignmentId: assignment?.id,\\r\\n        durationMinutes: drill.estimated_minutes ?? undefined,\\r\\n        options: {\\r\\n          returnTo: launchReturnTo,\\r\\n          label: drill.title,\\r\\n          initialIndex: resumeIndex ?? undefined,\\r\\n        },\\r\\n      });\\r\\n    navigate(`/drill?${launch}${resumeRun ? `&run=${encodeURIComponent(resumeRun.id)}` : \'\'}`);\\r\\n  }, [assignment, drill, navigate, qbLoading, bootcampOverride, slug, resumeQids, resumeIndex, storedGuided, launchReturnTo, requestedRunId, guidedRuns, activeGuidedRun]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (loading) {\\r\\n    return <div className=\\"min-h-screen bg-background text-foreground p-8\\">Loading...</div>;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (!drill && !bootcampOverride) {\\r\\n    return <div className=\\"min-h-screen bg-background text-foreground p-8\\">Guided drill not found.</div>;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Reopening a drill they already finished: show what they did. The question\\r\\n  // bank has to be loaded first, since the scorecard resolves each qid through\\r\\n  // it — without that gate the rows render blank for a moment.\\r\\n  if (runsLoading && !bootcampOverride) {\\r\\n    return <div className=\\"min-h-screen bg-background text-foreground p-8\\" role=\\"status\\">Loading your guided drill sittings…</div>;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  if ((completedGuidedRuns.length > 0 || legacyCompletedVisible) && !retaking && !activeGuidedRun && !visibleStoredGuided && !qbLoading) {\\r\\n    if (showLegacyResults && legacyCompletedVisible) return (\\r\\n      <PracticeSetResults\\r\\n        session={completedSession as DrillSession}\\r\\n        title={drill?.title ?? \'Your results\'}\\r\\n        subtitle=\\"Earlier guided drill sitting\\"\\r\\n        onBack={() => setShowLegacyResults(false)}\\r\\n        onRetry={() => { setRetaking(true); void handleStart(); }}\\r\\n      />\\r\\n    );\\r\\n    if (completedGuidedRuns.length > 0) return (\\r\\n      <main className=\\"mx-auto max-w-3xl space-y-5 px-5 py-8\\">\\r\\n        <Button variant=\\"ghost\\" onClick={handleReturn}>Back</Button>\\r\\n        <h1 className=\\"text-2xl font-semibold\\">{drill?.title ?? \'Guided drill\'}</h1>\\r\\n        <p className=\\"text-muted-foreground\\">Each completed sitting is saved separately. Open any one to see its results and answers.</p>\\r\\n        <div className=\\"space-y-3\\">{completedGuidedRuns.map((run) => (\\r\\n          <div key={run.id} className=\\"flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-4\\">\\r\\n            <div><strong>{new Date(run.updated_at).toLocaleDateString()}</strong><p className=\\"text-sm text-muted-foreground\\">{run.session.attempts.length} answered</p></div>\\r\\n            <Button variant=\\"outline\\" onClick={() => navigate(`/practice/drills/runs/${run.id}?view=results`)}>Results & review</Button>\\r\\n          </div>\\r\\n        ))}{legacyCompletedVisible && <div className=\\"flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-4\\">\\r\\n          <div><strong>Earlier sitting</strong><p className=\\"text-sm text-muted-foreground\\">Saved before individual sittings were available</p></div>\\r\\n          <Button variant=\\"outline\\" onClick={() => setShowLegacyResults(true)}>Results & review</Button>\\r\\n        </div>}</div>\\r\\n        <Button onClick={() => { setRetaking(true); void handleStart(); }}>Start a new sitting</Button>\\r\\n      </main>\\r\\n    );\\r\\n    const finishedOn = completedGuided?.updated_at\\r\\n      ? new Date(completedGuided.updated_at).toLocaleDateString(undefined, {\\r\\n          month: \'long\',\\r\\n          day: \'numeric\',\\r\\n        })\\r\\n      : null;\\r\\n    return (\\r\\n      <PracticeSetResults\\r\\n        session={completedSession as DrillSession}\\r\\n        title={drill?.title ?? \'Your results\'}\\r\\n        subtitle={\\r\\n          finishedOn\\r\\n            ? `You completed this on ${finishedOn}. A fresh attempt draws a new set of questions.`\\r\\n            : \'A fresh attempt draws a new set of questions.\'\\r\\n        }\\r\\n        onBack={handleReturn}\\r\\n        onRetry={() => {\\r\\n          setRetaking(true);\\r\\n          void handleStart();\\r\\n        }}\\r\\n      />\\r\\n    );\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  // No curated HTML launch page for this drill — build the identical premium\\r\\n  // launch page from the drill\'s stored instruction_card content so every drill\\r\\n  // renders in the same format (serif hero, black START card, notes drawer).\\r\\n  if (!launchPage) {\\r\\n    if (!drill) {\\r\\n      return <div className=\\"min-h-screen bg-background text-foreground p-8\\">Guided drill not found.</div>;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    // The assignment wins over the drill. A pinned set is exactly as long as\\r\\n    // the pin, and a trimmed assignment is as long as the teacher set it to;\\r\\n    // reading the drill\'s own count first advertises ten questions to a\\r\\n    // student who is about to be given six, which they notice.\\r\\n    const count =\\r\\n      assignment?.question_qids?.length ||\\r\\n      assignment?.question_count ||\\r\\n      drill.question_qids?.length ||\\r\\n      drill.question_count ||\\r\\n      fallbackPoolSize ||\\r\\n      0;\\r\\n\\r\\n    const html = buildGuidedDrillLaunchHtml({\\r\\n      title: drill.title,\\r\\n      estimatedMinutes: drill.estimated_minutes,\\r\\n      questionCount: count,\\r\\n      leftMd: drill.instruction_card?.left_md ?? null,\\r\\n      rightMd: drill.instruction_card?.right_md ?? null,\\r\\n    });\\r\\n\\r\\n    return (\\r\\n      <GuidedDrillLaunchFrame\\r\\n        html={html}\\r\\n        title={drill.title}\\r\\n        onStart={() => void handleStart()}\\r\\n        onReturn={handleReturn}\\r\\n        resumeNotice={resumeNotice}\\r\\n      />\\r\\n    );\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <GuidedDrillLaunchFrame\\r\\n      src={launchPage.htmlPath}\\r\\n      title={launchPage.title}\\r\\n      onStart={() => void handleStart()}\\r\\n      onReturn={handleReturn}\\r\\n      resumeNotice={resumeNotice}\\r\\n    />\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"6ee6eb","wall_time_seconds":1.6187049999999998,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1895,"output":"/**\\r\\n * Turning a Drill Launch\'s *name* into the questions it stands for.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * A launch names a run rather than containing it, so the kinds named by a row\\r\\n * have to read that row before the runner can start. This is the read.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * A row that is gone is a dead link, and a dead link says so. It does not fall\\r\\n * back to a generic drill: a student quietly drilling something nobody assigned\\r\\n * is worse than a student telling you a link is broken. See\\r\\n * `docs/adr/0016-a-drill-link-names-a-run-it-does-not-contain-one.md`.\\r\\n */\\r\\n\\r\\nimport { DrillLaunchError, launchNeedsResolution, type DrillLaunch } from \'@/lib/drillLaunch\';\\r\\nimport { listSavedSets } from \'@/lib/savedSets\';\\r\\nimport { supabase } from \'@/integrations/supabase/client\';\\r\\nimport { getPracticePool } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/data/practiceDrillPools\';\\r\\nimport {\\r\\n  pickHomeworkForDay,\\r\\n  pickPracticeForDay,\\r\\n} from \'@/bootcamps/principle-flaw/lib/dayQuestions\';\\r\\nimport {\\r\\n  drillResumeKey,\\r\\n  isResumableDrillSession,\\r\\n  loadDrillResumeSession,\\r\\n} from \'@/lib/drillResumeSession\';\\r\\nimport { getRecentExposureSet } from \'@/lib/questionExposure\';\\r\\nimport {\\r\\n  getAssignment,\\r\\n  getGuidedDrill,\\r\\n  resolveGuidedDrillQidsForLevel,\\r\\n  type GuidedDrill,\\r\\n  type GuidedDrillAssignment,\\r\\n} from \'@/lib/guidedDrills\';\\r\\nimport {\\r\\n  getLocalGuidedDrillBySlug,\\r\\n  isEmptyBankFilter,\\r\\n} from \'@/lib/guidedDrillsLocal\';\\r\\nimport {\\r\\n  guidedSessionKey,\\r\\n  loadGuidedDrillSession,\\r\\n} from \'@/lib/guidedDrillSession\';\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Raised when the link is well-formed but the thing it names is gone. */\\r\\nexport class DeadDrillLinkError extends DrillLaunchError {\\r\\n  constructor(message: string) {\\r\\n    super(message);\\r\\n    this.name = \'DeadDrillLinkError\';\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport async function resolveDrillLaunch(\\r\\n  launch: DrillLaunch,\\r\\n  userId: string | undefined,\\r\\n): Promise<string[] | undefined> {\\r\\n  if (!launchNeedsResolution(launch)) return undefined;\\r\\n\\r\\n  switch (launch.kind) {\\r\\n    case \'saved-set\': {\\r\\n      if (!userId) {\\r\\n        throw new DeadDrillLinkError(\'You need to be signed in to open a saved set.\');\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      // Saved sets belong to the student who saved them, so a link to someone\\r\\n      // else\'s set finds nothing here — which is the intended answer, not a\\r\\n      // failure to look hard enough.\\r\\n      const sets = await listSavedSets(userId);\\r\\n      const set = sets.find((s) => s.id === launch.setId);\\r\\n      if (!set) {\\r\\n        throw new DeadDrillLinkError(\'That saved set no longer exists.\');\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      if (set.question_ids.length === 0) {\\r\\n        throw new DeadDrillLinkError(\'That saved set has no questions in it.\');\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      return set.question_ids;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    case \'assignment\': {\\r\\n      // `homework_assignments`, which is where the classroom assignment surfaces\\r\\n      // read theirs from (`useStudentAssignment`). NOT `guided_drill_assignments`:\\r\\n      // those are a different table with different ids, and looking an id up in\\r\\n      // the wrong one makes a live assignment read as a dead link.\\r\\n      const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n        .from(\'homework_assignments\')\\r\\n        .select(\'id, question_qids\')\\r\\n        .eq(\'id\', launch.assignmentId)\\r\\n        .maybeSingle();\\r\\n      if (error) throw error;\\r\\n      const qids = (data as { question_qids?: string[] } | null)?.question_qids ?? [];\\r\\n      if (!data) {\\r\\n        throw new DeadDrillLinkError(\'That assignment no longer exists.\');\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      if (qids.length === 0) {\\r\\n        throw new DeadDrillLinkError(\'That assignment has no questions on it.\');\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      return qids;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    case \'practice-set\': {\\r\\n      // The pool is static source data with a fixed list, so re-resolving the\\r\\n      // slug gives back the same questions every time. Its Run Record is keyed\\r\\n      // by the slug too, so both safety conditions hold.\\r\\n      const pool = getPracticePool(launch.slug);\\r\\n      if (!pool) {\\r\\n        throw new DeadDrillLinkError(\'That practice set no longer exists.\');\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      return [...pool.qids];\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    case \'bootcamp-day\': {\\r\\n      const resumeKey = drillResumeKey({\\r\\n        mode: \'type-drill\',\\r\\n        identity: launch,\\r\\n      });\\r\\n      let hasIdentityRecord = false;\\r\\n      if (userId) {\\r\\n        const stored = await loadDrillResumeSession(userId, resumeKey);\\r\\n        hasIdentityRecord = !!stored;\\r\\n        if (isResumableDrillSession(stored)) return stored.question_queue;\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      // The day picker includes the student\'s recently-seen cooldown, so a\\r\\n      // fresh resolution can legitimately produce a different list. The\\r\\n      // identity-keyed Run Record above wins whenever one exists; only a new\\r\\n      // run reaches this draw. The gate waits for the question bank first.\\r\\n      const excludedQids = userId ? await getRecentExposureSet(userId, 500) : new Set<string>();\\r\\n      const qids =\\r\\n        launch.part === \'homework\'\\r\\n          ? pickHomeworkForDay(launch.day, undefined, excludedQids)\\r\\n          : pickPracticeForDay(launch.day, undefined, excludedQids);\\r\\n      if (userId && !hasIdentityRecord && qids.length > 0) {\\r\\n        const legacyKey = drillResumeKey({\\r\\n          mode: \'type-drill\',\\r\\n          config: { selectedQids: qids },\\r\\n        });\\r\\n        const legacy = await loadDrillResumeSession(userId, legacyKey);\\r\\n        if (isResumableDrillSession(legacy)) return legacy.question_queue;\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      if (qids.length === 0) {\\r\\n        throw new DeadDrillLinkError(\\r\\n          `Day ${launch.day} has no ${launch.part} questions to give yet.`,\\r\\n        );\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      return qids;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    case \'guided-drill\': {\\r\\n      let assignment: (GuidedDrillAssignment & { guided_drill?: GuidedDrill }) | null = null;\\r\\n      let drill: GuidedDrill | null = null;\\r\\n\\r\\n      if (launch.assignmentId) {\\r\\n        assignment = (await getAssignment(launch.assignmentId)) as typeof assignment;\\r\\n        drill = assignment?.guided_drill ?? null;\\r\\n      } else if (launch.drillId) {\\r\\n        drill = await getGuidedDrill(launch.drillId);\\r\\n        if (!drill) drill = getLocalGuidedDrillBySlug(launch.drillId);\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      if (!drill) {\\r\\n        throw new DeadDrillLinkError(\'That guided drill no longer exists.\');\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      const local = getLocalGuidedDrillBySlug(drill.slug);\\r\\n      if (local && isEmptyBankFilter(drill.bank_filter)) {\\r\\n        drill = {\\r\\n          ...drill,\\r\\n          bank_filter: local.bank_filter,\\r\\n          question_count: drill.question_count ?? local.question_count,\\r\\n        };\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      if (userId) {\\r\\n        const currentKey = guidedSessionKey({\\r\\n          assignmentId: launch.assignmentId,\\r\\n          drillId: launch.assignmentId ? undefined : launch.drillId,\\r\\n        });\\r\\n        const legacyKey = launch.assignmentId\\r\\n          ? null\\r\\n          : guidedSessionKey({ slug: drill.slug });\\r\\n        const stored = await loadGuidedDrillSession(userId, currentKey, legacyKey);\\r\\n        if (\\r\\n          stored?.status === \'in_progress\' &&\\r\\n          stored.question_queue.length > 0 &&\\r\\n          stored.attempts.length > 0\\r\\n        ) {\\r\\n          return stored.question_queue;\\r\\n        }\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      const resolution = resolveGuidedDrillQidsForLevel({ drill, assignment });\\r\\n      if (resolution.warning === \'empty\' || resolution.qids.length === 0) {\\r\\n        throw new DeadDrillLinkError(\'That guided drill has no questions to give yet.\');\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      return resolution.qids;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    default:\\r\\n      throw new DrillLaunchError(\\r\\n        `${launch.kind} links are not accepted yet — open it from the classroom instead.`,\\r\\n      );\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]

## assistant

Implemented guided resume in four files:

- Restores the owned sitting’s frozen queue before drawing from the current pool.
- Checks current source assignment ownership and matching drill identity first.
- Rejects missing or mismatched requested sittings without silently starting fresh.
- Excludes superseded legacy copies and keys gate resolution by account and sitting.

30 focused tests passed. Changed-file ESLint passed.

Browser verification remains for the parent before release. No backend mutations, commits, or pushes.

Your device copy has been kept. Reload the exam to continue.\');\\r\\n    this.name = \'PracticeTestConflictError\';\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Stable in one browser, different on another device or browser profile. */\\r\\nexport function getPracticeTestDeviceId(): string {\\r\\n  if (memoryDeviceId) return memoryDeviceId;\\r\\n  let id: string | null = null;\\r\\n  try { id = localStorage.getItem(DEVICE_KEY); } catch { /* storage disabled */ }\\r\\n  if (id) return (memoryDeviceId = id);\\r\\n  id = globalThis.crypto?.randomUUID?.() ?? `device-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`;\\r\\n  try { localStorage.setItem(DEVICE_KEY, id); } catch { /* revision still protects writes */ }\\r\\n  return (memoryDeviceId = id);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function assertAccount(userId: string): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.getUser();\\r\\n  if (error || !data.user || data.user.id !== userId) {\\r\\n    throw new Error(\'Your account session changed. []) as RcAttemptRow[]).map(toAttempt);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Sets the flag on every stored attempt for a question in a session. */\\r\\nexport async function setAttemptFlag(\\r\\n  sessionId: string,\\r\\n  questionId: number,\\r\\n  flagged: boolean,\\r\\n): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  await assertActivePracticeTestSession(sessionId);\\r\\n  const { error } = await rcDb\\r\\n    .from(RC_ATTEMPTS_TABLE)\\r\\n    .update({ flagged })\\r\\n    .eq(\'session_id\', sessionId)\\r\\n    .eq(\'question_id\', questionId);\\r\\n  raise(error);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Per-question aggregate over the student\'s whole history, used by the hub\'s\\r\\n * status filters and by adaptive selection.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport async function getQuestionHistory(userId?: string): Promise<RcQuestionHistory> {\\r\\n  const uid = userId ?? (await requireUserId());\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await rcDb\\r\\n    .from(RC_ATTEMPTS_TABLE)\\r\\n    .select(\'question_id, is_correct, created_at\')\\r\\n    .eq(\'user_id\', uid)\\r\\n    .order(\'created_at\', { ascending: true });\\r\\n  raise(error);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const history: RcQuestionHistory = new Map();\\r\\n  for (const row of (data ?? []) as Pick<\\r\\n    RcAttemptRow,\\r\\n    \'question_id\' | \'is_correct\' | \'created_at\'\\r\\n  >[]) {\\r\\n    const existing = history.get(row.question_id) ?? {\\r\\n      questionId: row.question_id,\\r\\n      attempts: 0,\\r\\n      everIncorrect: false,\\r\\n      incorrectOnLastTake: false,\\r\\n      incorrectCount: 0,\\r\\n      lastSeenAt: null as string | null,\\r\\n    };\\r\\n    const wrong = row.is_correct === false;\\r\\n    existing.attempts += 1;\\r\\n    if (wrong) {\\r\\n      existing.everIncorrect = true;\\r\\n      existing.incorrectCount += 1;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    // rows arrive oldest first, so the last one seen decides the latest take\\r\\n    existing.incorrectOnLastTake = wrong;\\r\\n    existing.lastSeenAt = row.created_at;\\r\\n    history.set(row.question_id, existing);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return history;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------- annotations */\\r\\n\\r\\nexport async function loadAnnotations(\\r\\n  passageIds: number[],\\r\\n): Promise<Map<number, RcHighlight[]>> {\\r\\n  const result = new Map<number, RcHighlight[]>();\\r\\n  if (passageIds.length === 0) return result;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await rcDb\\r\\n    .from(RC_ANNOTATIONS_TABLE)\\r\\n    .select(\'passage_id, highlights\')\\r\\n    .in(\'passage_id\', passageIds);\\r\\n  raise(error);\\r\\n\\r\\n  for (const row of (data ?? []) as { passage_id: number; highlights: unknown }[]) {\\r\\n    result.set(row.passage_id, (row.highlights ?? []) as RcHighlight[]);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return result;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst saveTimers = new Map<number, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>();\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Debounced by one second per passage, so dragging out a highlight writes once. */\\r\\nexport function saveAnnotations(passageId: number, highlights: RcHighlight[]): void {\\r\\n  const pending = saveTimers.get(passageId);\\r\\n  if (pending) clearTimeout(pending);\\r\\n  saveTimers.set(\\r\\n    passageId,\\r\\n    setTimeout(() => {\\r\\n      saveTimers.delete(passageId);\\r\\n      void saveAnnotationsNow(passageId, highlights);\\r\\n    }, 1000),\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport async function saveAnnotationsNow(\\r\\n  passageId: number,\\r\\n  highlights: RcHighlight[],\\r\\n): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  const userId = await requireUserId();\\r\\n  const { error } = await rcDb.from(RC_ANNOTATIONS_TABLE).upsert(\\r\\n    {\\r\\n      user_id: userId,\\r\\n      passage_id: passageId,\\r\\n      highlights,\\r\\n      updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),\\r\\n    },\\r\\n    { onConflict: \'user_id,passage_id\' },\\r\\n  );\\r\\n  raise(error);\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"ebd155","wall_time_seconds":2.3372631999999998,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1285,"output":"/**\\r\\n * Resume state for every OTHER drill — Smart Drill, custom type drills, and\\r\\n * full sections.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Guided drills and Intro to LR practice sets already survive a closed tab, a\\r\\n * sign-out, or a different device (see guidedDrillSession). Nothing else did:\\r\\n * a student mid-way through a Smart Drill who closed the browser came back to\\r\\n * a fresh shuffle, and the answers they had already given were nowhere on the\\r\\n * screen. The attempts themselves were never lost — those are written to the\\r\\n * `attempts` table per question — but the run was, which reads as lost work.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * This rides the exact same store as the other two, namespaced so the three can\\r\\n * never collide:\\r\\n *\\r\\n *     practice set   →  pool_slug \\"mc-practice-1\\"\\r\\n *     guided drill   →  pool_slug \\"guided::<assignmentId>\\"\\r\\n *     bootcamp day   →  pool_slug \\"drill::bootcamp-day::<day>::<part>\\"\\r\\n *     homework       →  pool_slug \\"drill::assignment::<assignmentId>\\"\\r\\n *     everything else→  pool_slug \\"drill::<mode>::<fingerprint>\\"\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Official section reads must confirm the account or retain a paused device\\r\\n * copy. Other drill reads preserve the legacy missing-row fallback.\\r\\n */\\r\\n\\r\\nimport {\\r\\n  loadPracticeSession,\\r\\n  savePracticeSessionSnapshot,\\r\\n  completePracticeSession,\\r\\n  migratePracticeSession,\\r\\n  practiceSessionIdFor,\\r\\n  PRACTICE_SESSION_DATA_VERSION,\\r\\n  type PracticeSessionSnapshot,\\r\\n} from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/lib/practiceSessionStore\';\\r\\nimport { PracticeSession, TABLES } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/persistence/records\';\\r\\nimport { supabase } from \'@/integrations/supabase/client\';\\r\\nimport { isActivePracticeTestDevice } from \'@/lib/practiceTestStore\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport const DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX = \'drill::\';\\r\\nconst SECTION_PREFIX = `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}section::`;\\r\\nconst TEST_LEG_PREFIX = `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}practice-test::`;\\r\\nconst accountSectionKey = (key: string) =>\\r\\n  key.startsWith(SECTION_PREFIX) || key.startsWith(TEST_LEG_PREFIX);\\r\\nconst accountWriteChains = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction queueAccountWrite(userId: string, key: string, write: () => Promise<void>): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  const identity = `${userId}::${key}`;\\r\\n  const previous = accountWriteChains.get(identity) ?? Promise.resolve();\\r\\n  const current = previous.catch(() => undefined).then(write);\\r\\n  accountWriteChains.set(identity, current);\\r\\n  void current.finally(() => {\\r\\n    if (accountWriteChains.get(identity) === current) accountWriteChains.delete(identity);\\r\\n  }).catch(() => undefined);\\r\\n  return current;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function assertActiveTestLeg(userId: string, key: string): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  if (!key.startsWith(TEST_LEG_PREFIX)) return;\\r\\n  const runId = key.slice(TEST_LEG_PREFIX.length).split(\'::\')[0];\\r\\n  if (!await isActivePracticeTestDevice(userId, runId)) {\\r\\n    throw new Error(\'This practice test is active on another device.\');\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function readSectionFromAccount(userId: string, key: string): Promise<PracticeSession | null> {\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.from(\'bootcamp_kv\')\\r\\n    .select(\'data\')\\r\\n    .eq(\'user_id\', userId)\\r\\n    .eq(\'table_name\', TABLES.practice_sessions)\\r\\n    .eq(\'record_id\', practiceSessionIdFor(userId, key))\\r\\n    .maybeSingle();\\r\\n  if (error) throw error;\\r\\n  const parsed = PracticeSession.safeParse(data?.data);\\r\\n  return parsed.success ? migratePracticeSession(parsed.data) : null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function syncSectionToAccount(userId: string, key: string): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  const record = await loadPracticeSession(userId, key);\\r\\n  if (!record) throw new Error(\'The section could not be saved.\');\\r\\n  const { error } = await supabase.from(\'bootcamp_kv\').upsert({\\r\\n    user_id: userId,\\r\\n    table_name: TABLES.practice_sessions,\\r\\n    record_id: record.id,\\r\\n    data: record,\\r\\n  }, { onConflict: \'user_id,table_name,record_id\' });\\r\\n  if (error) throw error;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type DrillResumeSession = PracticeSession;\\r\\nexport type DrillResumeSnapshot = PracticeSessionSnapshot;\\r\\n\\r\\nconst sectionBackupKey = (userId: string, key: string) =>\\r\\n  `lsatu:section-backup:${practiceSessionIdFor(userId, key)}`;\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Synchronous device copy, including the latest unacknowledged choice. */\\r\\nexport function writeSectionDeviceBackup(\\r\\n  userId: string,\\r\\n  key: string | null,\\r\\n  snapshot: DrillResumeSnapshot,\\r\\n): string | null {\\r\\n  if (!userId || !key || !accountSectionKey(key) || !snapshot.questionQueue.length) return null;\\r\\n  const previous = readSectionDeviceBackup(userId, key);\\r\\n  const time = new Date(Math.max(Date.now(), previous ? Date.parse(previous.updated_at) + 1 : 0)).toISOString();\\r\\n  const record = PracticeSession.safeParse({\\r\\n    id: practiceSessionIdFor(userId, key),\\r\\n    user_id: userId,\\r\\n    pool_slug: key,\\r\\n    completion_id: key,\\r\\n    data_version: PRACTICE_SESSION_DATA_VERSION,\\r\\n    question_queue: snapshot.questionQueue,\\r\\n    current_index: Math.min(Math.max(snapshot.currentIndex, 0), snapshot.questionQueue.length - 1),\\r\\n    attempts: snapshot.attempts,\\r\\n    status: \'in_progress\',\\r\\n    elapsed_ms: snapshot.elapsedMs,\\r\\n    timer_mode: snapshot.timerMode,\\r\\n    timer_custom_minutes: snapshot.timerCustomMinutes,\\r\\n"}'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 8.9 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"ae91d7","wall_time_seconds":2.5538672,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":3393,"output":"-- Practice-test runs live in the database, and legs are tagged rather than guessed.\\r\\n--\\r\\n-- Until now a sitting existed only in one browser\'s localStorage. Clearing site\\r\\n-- data lost it, a second device could not see it, and the scorer found a leg by\\r\\n-- matching \\"this user, this section, finished after the leg began\\", which is a\\r\\n-- guess that gets less true the more a student drills.\\r\\n--\\r\\n-- This migration is the schema half of that change. It is written to converge\\r\\n-- from any starting point: every statement is guarded, so a clean replay and a\\r\\n-- re-run against the live database both end at the same schema.\\r\\n--\\r\\n-- ORDER IS LOAD-BEARING in 1a. `alter table ... add column ... default` fills\\r\\n-- every existing row with the default, so adding the default before the backfill\\r\\n-- would overwrite real `section_mode` values and real completion times. Add the\\r\\n-- columns bare, backfill from the truth already in the row, and only then attach\\r\\n-- the defaults for future inserts.\\r\\n\\r\\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\r\\n-- 1a. Normalize section_history\\r\\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\r\\n\\r\\nalter table public.section_history\\r\\n  add column if not exists mode text,\\r\\n  add column if not exists completed_at timestamptz,\\r\\n  add column if not exists user_id uuid references auth.users (id) on delete cascade;\\r\\n\\r\\nupdate public.section_history\\r\\n   set mode = coalesce(mode, section_mode, \'full-section\')\\r\\n where mode is null;\\r\\n\\r\\nupdate public.section_history\\r\\n   set completed_at = created_at\\r\\n where completed_at is null;\\r\\n\\r\\nalter table public.section_history alter column mode set default \'full-section\';\\r\\nalter table public.section_history alter column completed_at set default now();\\r\\nalter table public.section_history alter column section_mode drop not null;\\r\\n\\r\\n-- The INSERT policy has to be REPLACED, not supplemented. PostgreSQL ORs\\r\\n-- permissive policies together, so leaving the old class-only rule in place\\r\\n-- would keep a live bypass around the new ownership test, and the composite FK\\r\\n-- further down would be decorative. The legacy policies are dropped by role\\r\\n-- rather than by name because their names differ across environments; this\\r\\n-- drops exactly the INSERT policies (\'a\' = ALTER/append = INSERT in pg_policy)\\r\\n-- and leaves SELECT/UPDATE/DELETE untouched.\\r\\ndo $$\\r\\ndeclare\\r\\n  pol record;\\r\\nbegin\\r\\n  for pol in\\r\\n    select polname\\r\\n      from pg_policy\\r\\n     where polrelid = \'public.section_history\'::regclass\\r\\n       and polcmd = \'a\'\\r\\n  loop\\r\\n    execute format(\\r\\n      \'drop policy if exists %I on public.section_history\', pol.polname\\r\\n    );\\r\\n  end loop;\\r\\nend $$;\\r\\n\\r\\ncreate policy \\"section_history_owner_insert\\" on public.section_history\\r\\n  for insert with check (\\r\\n    user_id = auth.uid()\\r\\n    and class_id = public.get_user_class_id(auth.uid())\\r\\n  );\\r\\n\\r\\n-- If this returns anything other than one row, an insert path was reintroduced\\r\\n-- somewhere and the ownership guarantee is gone:\\r\\n--   select polname from pg_policy\\r\\n--    where polrelid = \'public.section_history\'::regclass and polcmd = \'a\';\\r\\n\\r\\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\r\\n-- 1b. The runs table\\r\\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\r\\n\\r\\n-- id is text, not uuid: newRunId()\'s fallback path does not produce a uuid.\\r\\ncreate table if not exists public.practice_test_runs (\\r\\n  id               text primary key,\\r\\n  user_id          uuid not null default auth.uid() references auth.users (id) on delete cascade,\\r\\n  pt               int not null,\\r\\n  pace             text not null,\\r\\n  legs             jsonb not null,\\r\\n  current_leg      int not null default 0,\\r\\n  break_after_leg  int not null,\\r\\n  break_taken      boolean not null default false,\\r\\n  break_started_at timestamptz,\\r\\n  status           text not null check (status in (\'in-progress\', \'complete\', \'abandoned\')),\\r\\n  revision         int not null default 0,\\r\\n  completed_at     timestamptz,\\r\\n  raw_correct      int,\\r\\n  raw_total        int,\\r\\n  scaled_score     int,\\r\\n  score_source     text check (score_source in (\'official-table\', \'estimate\')),\\r\\n  created_at       timestamptz not null default now(),\\r\\n  updated_at       timestamptz not null default now(),\\r\\n  -- Exists solely as the target of the composite FKs below. It is what makes\\r\\n  -- cross-user leg attribution impossible at the schema level rather than by\\r\\n  -- convention.\\r\\n  unique (id, user_id)\\r\\n);\\r\\n\\r\\ncreate index if not exists practice_test_runs_user_status\\r\\n  on public.practice_test_runs (user_id, status);\\r\\n\\r\\nalter table public.practice_test_runs enable row level security;\\r\\n\\r\\n-- No set_updated_at / handle_updated_at function exists in this project\'s\\r\\n-- migrations, so it is created here rather than reused.\\r\\ncreate or replace function public.touch_practice_test_runs()\\r\\nreturns trigger\\r\\nlanguage plpgsql\\r\\nset search_path = public\\r\\nas $$\\r\\nbegin\\r\\n  new.updated_at = now();\\r\\n  return new;\\r\\nend;\\r\\n$$;\\r\\n\\r\\ndrop trigger if exists practice_test_runs_touch on public.practice_test_runs;\\r\\ncreate trigger practice_test_runs_touch\\r\\n  before update on public.practice_test_runs\\r\\n  for each row execute function public.touch_practice_test_runs();\\r\\n\\r\\ndrop policy if exists \\"pt_runs_owner_select\\" on public.practice_test_runs;\\r\\ncreate policy \\"pt_runs_owner_select\\" on public.practice_test_runs\\r\\n  for select using (user_id = auth.uid());\\r\\n\\r\\ndrop policy if exists \\"pt_runs_owner_insert\\" on public.practice_test_runs;\\r\\ncreate policy \\"pt_runs_owner_insert\\" on public.practice_test_runs\\r\\n  for insert with check (user_id = auth.uid());\\r\\n\\r\\ndrop policy if exists \\"pt_runs_owner_update\\" on public.practice_test_runs;\\r\\ncreate policy \\"pt_runs_owner_update\\" on public.practice_test_runs\\r\\n  for update using (user_id = auth.uid()) with check (user_id = auth.uid());\\r\\n\\r\\ndrop policy if exists \\"pt_runs_admin_select\\" on public.practice_test_runs;\\r\\ncreate policy \\"pt_runs_admin_select\\" on public.practice_test_runs\\r\\n  for select using (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\\r\\n\\r\\n-- Deliberately NO delete policy, for anyone. A sitting is a record of work.\\r\\n-- Terminal-state protection beyond RLS is enforced client-side by\\r\\n-- status-filtered conditional updates; a hostile owner editing their own row\\r\\n-- can corrupt only their own history, which is acceptable here.\\r\\n\\r\\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\r\\n-- 1c. Leg attribution\\r\\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\r\\n\\r\\nalter table public.section_history\\r\\n  add column if not exists practice_test_run_id text,\\r\\n  add column if not exists practice_test_leg int;\\r\\n\\r\\nalter table public.rc_drill_sessions\\r\\n  add column if not exists practice_test_run_id text,\\r\\n  add column if not exists practice_test_leg int;\\r\\n\\r\\n-- `add constraint` has no IF NOT EXISTS, so each is guarded by catalog lookup.\\r\\ndo $$\\r\\nbegin\\r\\n  if not exists (select 1 from pg_constraint\\r\\n                  where conname = \'section_history_pt_leg_chk\'\\r\\n                    and conrelid = \'public.section_history\'::regclass) then\\r\\n    alter table public.section_history\\r\\n      add constraint section_history_pt_leg_chk check (practice_test_leg >= 0);\\r\\n  end if;\\r\\n\\r\\n  -- Closes the run-id-without-leg hole in both directions.\\r\\n  if not exists (select 1 from pg_constraint\\r\\n                  where conname = \'section_history_pt_pair_chk\'\\r\\n                    and conrelid = \'public.section_history\'::regclass) then\\r\\n    alter table public.section_history\\r\\n      add constraint section_history_pt_pair_chk\\r\\n      check ((practice_test_run_id is null) = (practice_test_leg is null));\\r\\n  end if;\\r\\n\\r\\n  -- section_history.user_id is nullable, and PostgreSQL\'s default MATCH SIMPLE\\r\\n  -- skips composite-FK validation when ANY referencing column is null. Without\\r\\n  -- this an attributed row with a null user_id sails past the FK below and\\r\\n  -- consumes a run-leg slot it does not own.\\r\\n  if not exists (select 1 from pg_constraint\\r\\n                  where conname = \'section_history_attr_owner_chk\'\\r\\n                    and conrelid = \'public.section_history\'::regclass) then\\r\\n    alter table public.section_history\\r\\n      add constraint section_history_attr_owner_chk\\r\\n      check (practice_test_run_id is null or user_id is not null);\\r\\n  end if;\\r\\n\\r\\n  if not exists (select 1 from pg_constraint\\r\\n                  where conname = \'section_history_pt_run_fk\'\\r\\n                    and conrelid = \'public.section_history\'::regclass) then\\r\\n    alter table public.section_history\\r\\n      add constraint section_history_pt_run_fk\\r\\n      foreign key (practice_test_run_id, user_id)\\r\\n      references public.practice_test_runs (id, user_id);\\r\\n  end if;\\r\\n\\r\\n  if not exists (select 1 from pg_constraint\\r\\n                  where conname = \'rc_sessions_pt_leg_chk\'\\r\\n                    and conrelid = \'public.rc_drill_sessions\'::regclass) then\\r\\n    alter table public.rc_drill_sessions\\r\\n      add constraint rc_sessions_pt_leg_chk check (practice_test_leg >= 0);\\r\\n  end if;\\r\\n\\r\\n  if not exists (select 1 from pg_constraint\\r\\n                  where conname = \'rc_sessions_pt_pair_chk\'\\r\\n                    and conrelid = \'public.rc_drill_sessions\'::regclass) then\\r\\n    alter table public.rc_drill_sessions\\r\\n      add constraint rc_sessions_pt_pair_chk\\r\\n      check ((practice_test_run_id is null) = (practice_test_leg is null));\\r\\n  end if;\\r\\n\\r\\n  if not exists (select 1 from pg_constraint\\r\\n                  where conname = \'rc_sessions_pt_run_fk\'\\r\\n                    and conrelid = \'public.rc_drill_sessions\'::regclass) then\\r\\n    alter table public.rc_drill_sessions\\r\\n      add constraint rc_sessions_pt_run_fk\\r\\n      foreign key (practice_test_run_id, user_id)\\r\\n      references public.practice_test_runs (id, user_id);\\r\\n  end if;\\r\\n\\r\\n  -- rc_attempts references a session by session_id alone, while its RLS checks\\r\\n  -- only the attempt\'s own user_id, so attempts can be attached to another\\r\\n  -- user\'s session. The composite FK below closes that, and needs this target.\\r\\n  if not exists (select 1 from pg_constraint\\r\\n                  where conname = \'rc_drill_sessions_id_user_key\'\\r\\n                    and conrelid = \'public.rc_drill_sessions\'::regclass) then\\r\\n    alter table public.rc_drill_sessions\\r\\n      add constraint rc_drill_sessions_id_user_key unique (id, user_id);\\r\\n  end if;\\r\\n\\r\\n  if not exists (select 1 from pg_constraint\\r\\n                  where conname = \'rc_attempts_session_owner_fk\'\\r\\n                    and conrelid = \'public.rc_attempts\'::regclass) then\\r\\n    alter table public.rc_attempts\\r\\n      add constraint rc_attempts_session_owner_fk\\r\\n      foreign key (session_id, user_id)\\r\\n      references public.rc_drill_sessions (id, user_id);\\r\\n  end if;\\r\\nend $$;\\r\\n\\r\\n-- One row per leg, forever. This is what makes a retried closeout safe: a\\r\\n-- duplicate insert fails with 23505 instead of scoring the leg twice.\\r\\ncreate unique index if not exists section_history_pt_leg_once\\r\\n  on public.section_history (practice_test_run_id, practice_test_leg)\\r\\n  where practice_test_run_id is not null;\\r\\n\\r\\ncreate unique index if not exists rc_sessions_pt_leg_once\\r\\n  on public.rc_drill_sessions (practice_test_run_id, practice_test_leg)\\r\\n  where practice_test_run_id is not null;\\r\\n\\r\\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\r\\n-- 1d. RC first-attempt integrity\\r\\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\r\\n\\r\\n-- recordAttempt is read-then-insert with no constraint, so racing writes can\\r\\n-- already have double-counted. The unique index below will not build over those\\r\\n-- duplicates, so they are resolved first.\\r\\n--\\r\\n-- DEMOTE, do not delete. A duplicate is a real attempt the student made; only\\r\\n-- its is_first_attempt flag is wrong, and the review surfaces legitimately show\\r\\n-- it as a later attempt. Ranking is by (created_at, id) and never by ctid, which\\r\\n-- is physical row location rather than chronology and would retain an arbitrary\\r\\n-- row as \\"first\\".\\r\\nwith ranked as (\\r\\n  select id,\\r\\n         row_number() over (\\r\\n           partition by session_id, question_id\\r\\n           order by created_at asc, id asc\\r\\n         ) as rn\\r\\n    from public.rc_attempts\\r\\n   where is_first_attempt\\r\\n)\\r\\nupdate public.rc_attempts a\\r\\n   set is_first_attempt = false\\r\\n  from ranked r\\r\\n where a.id = r.id\\r\\n   and r.rn > 1;\\r\\n\\r\\ncreate unique index if not exists rc_attempts_first_once\\r\\n  on public.rc_attempts (session_id, question_id)\\r\\n  where is_first_attempt;\\r\\n\\r\\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\r\\n-- 1e. Stale-run cleanup (used by change 5, shipped here so one migration\\r\\n--     carries all the DDL)\\r\\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\r\\n\\r\\n-- exclude_ids exists for change 5. A run whose dirty retry failed still LOOKS\\r\\n-- stale, because its offline progress never landed to refresh updated_at.\\r\\n-- Abandoning it server-side would let the terminal-wins reconcile then destroy\\r\\n-- that progress, so change 5 passes those ids here. The default keeps a\\r\\n-- no-argument call valid.\\r\\ncreate or replace function public.abandon_stale_practice_tests(\\r\\n  exclude_ids text[] default \'{}\'\\r\\n)\\r\\nreturns setof text\\r\\nlanguage sql\\r\\nsecurity invoker\\r\\nset search_path = public\\r\\nas $$\\r\\n  update public.practice_test_runs\\r\\n     set status = \'abandoned\',\\r\\n         revision = revision + 1\\r\\n   where user_id = auth.uid()\\r\\n     and status = \'in-progress\'\\r\\n     and updated_at < now() - interval \'14 days\'\\r\\n     and not (id = any (coalesce(exclude_ids, \'{}\')))\\r\\n  returning id;\\r\\n$$;\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"e20619","wall_time_seconds":2.7562680999999998,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":168,"output":"-- Account-backed practice tests retain the timing chosen at creation and\\r\\n-- identify the browser currently allowed to advance the run.\\r\\nalter table public.practice_test_runs\\r\\n  add column if not exists section_seconds integer,\\r\\n  add column if not exists active_device_id text;\\r\\n\\r\\nalter table public.practice_test_runs\\r\\n  drop constraint if exists practice_test_runs_section_seconds_check;\\r\\nalter table public.practice_test_runs\\r\\n  add constraint practice_test_runs_section_seconds_check\\r\\n  check (section_seconds is null or section_seconds > 0);\\r\\n\\r\\ncreate index if not exists practice_test_runs_user_created\\r\\n  on public.practice_test_runs (user_id, created_at desc);\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"762039","wall_time_seconds":2.8086571,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":846,"output":"-- Assignable practice tests (access tiers, stage 7).\\r\\n--\\r\\n-- A practice test here is the full sitting of one PrepTest. Assigning PT 140 to\\r\\n-- a student opens that one sitting and nothing else: the Testing Centre lists\\r\\n-- only their assigned PrepTests unless they have the Practice tests switch.\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.practice_test_assignments (\\r\\n  id          uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\\r\\n  student_id  uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\\r\\n  pt          integer NOT NULL,\\r\\n  assigned_by uuid,\\r\\n  assigned_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\\r\\n  unlock_at   timestamptz,\\r\\n  status      text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'assigned\'\\r\\n              CHECK (status IN (\'assigned\', \'completed\', \'archived\')),\\r\\n  UNIQUE (student_id, pt)\\r\\n);\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS practice_test_assignments_student_idx\\r\\n  ON public.practice_test_assignments (student_id);\\r\\n\\r\\nALTER TABLE public.practice_test_assignments ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\\r\\n\\r\\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS \\"Admins manage practice test assignments\\" ON public.practice_test_assignments;\\r\\nCREATE POLICY \\"Admins manage practice test assignments\\"\\r\\n  ON public.practice_test_assignments FOR ALL\\r\\n  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'))\\r\\n  WITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\\r\\n\\r\\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS \\"Students read own practice test assignments\\" ON public.practice_test_assignments;\\r\\nCREATE POLICY \\"Students read own practice test assignments\\"\\r\\n  ON public.practice_test_assignments FOR SELECT\\r\\n  USING (\\r\\n    student_id = auth.uid()\\r\\n    AND status <> \'archived\'\\r\\n    AND (unlock_at IS NULL OR unlock_at <= now())\\r\\n  );\\r\\n\\r\\n-- True when this user may start a sitting of this PrepTest: the Practice tests\\r\\n-- switch, an admin, or a live assignment for exactly this PT.\\r\\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.can_sit_practice_test(_user uuid, _pt integer)\\r\\n RETURNS boolean\\r\\n LANGUAGE sql\\r\\n STABLE\\r\\n SECURITY DEFINER\\r\\n SET search_path TO \'public\'\\r\\nAS $function$\\r\\n  SELECT\\r\\n    public.has_role(_user, \'admin\')\\r\\n    OR EXISTS (\\r\\n      SELECT 1 FROM public.profiles\\r\\n      WHERE class_id = _user::text AND has_practice_access\\r\\n    )\\r\\n    OR EXISTS (\\r\\n      SELECT 1 FROM public.practice_test_assignments\\r\\n      WHERE student_id = _user AND pt = _pt AND status <> \'archived\'\\r\\n        AND (unlock_at IS NULL OR unlock_at <= now())\\r\\n    );\\r\\n$function$;\\r\\n\\r\\n-- The API cannot be used to start a sitting the student was not given.\\r\\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS pt_runs_owner_insert ON public.practice_test_runs;\\r\\nCREATE POLICY pt_runs_owner_insert\\r\\n  ON public.practice_test_runs FOR INSERT\\r\\n  WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid() AND public.can_sit_practice_test(auth.uid(), pt));\\r\\n\\r\\n-- An assigned sitting shows on Assigned work like everything else.\\r\\nALTER TABLE public.classroom_items DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS classroom_items_type_check;\\r\\nALTER TABLE public.classroom_items ADD CONSTRAINT classroom_items_type_check\\r\\n  CHECK (type = ANY (ARRAY[\'drill\', \'homework\', \'bootcamp_module\', \'note\', \'session_recap\',\\r\\n    \'announcement\', \'upload_summary\', \'weekly_plan_task\', \'smartboard_recap\', \'activity\',\\r\\n    \'practice_test\']));\\r\\n\\r\\n-- Explicit, though the schema\'s default privileges already grant these. RLS\\r\\n-- above decides which rows each role actually sees.\\r\\nGRANT SELECT ON public.practice_test_assignments TO authenticated;\\r\\nGRANT ALL ON public.practice_test_assignments TO service_role;\\r\\n"}'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 20.5 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"a0ef58","wall_time_seconds":3.1478724,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":476,"output":"-- Per-user key/value store for bootcamp progress autosave.\\r\\n-- The bootcamp persistence layer stores small JSON records keyed by\\r\\n-- (table_name, record_id). This mirrors them to the backend so each\\r\\n-- profile keeps all of their progress across devices/sessions.\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.bootcamp_kv (\\r\\n  user_id      uuid        NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\\r\\n  table_name   text        NOT NULL,\\r\\n  record_id    text        NOT NULL,\\r\\n  data         jsonb       NOT NULL,\\r\\n  updated_at   timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\\r\\n  PRIMARY KEY (user_id, table_name, record_id)\\r\\n);\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS bootcamp_kv_user_table_idx\\r\\n  ON public.bootcamp_kv (user_id, table_name);\\r\\n\\r\\nGRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.bootcamp_kv TO authenticated;\\r\\nGRANT ALL ON public.bootcamp_kv TO service_role;\\r\\n\\r\\nALTER TABLE public.bootcamp_kv ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE POLICY \\"Users read own bootcamp data\\"\\r\\n  ON public.bootcamp_kv FOR SELECT TO authenticated\\r\\n  USING (auth.uid() = user_id);\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE POLICY \\"Users insert own bootcamp data\\"\\r\\n  ON public.bootcamp_kv FOR INSERT TO authenticated\\r\\n  WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE POLICY \\"Users update own bootcamp Both frames now look too simple. The sleeping brain runs distinct programs in different stages, and interventions that disrupt one stage impair some functions while leaving others intact: slow-wave disruption degrades factual recall but spares motor learning, while dream-stage suppression does something closer to the reverse. Consolidation has not been dethroned as one genuine function among several. What has been abandoned is the search for the purpose of sleep, in favor of mapping which stage serves which function, on the view that sleep is a schedule of maintenance operations rather than a single process with a single point.\\", \\"credited_answers\\": [\\"Sleep science has moved from asking what sleep is for to mapping which sleep stage serves which function, treating sleep as a schedule of distinct maintenance programs, with memory consolidation kept as one function among several.\\"], \\"explanation\\": \\"The big ideas your summary has to carry: (1) frame one: idle brain · (2) frame two: one master task (consolidation) · (3) current view: stage-specific functions shown by selective disruption · (4) retained: consolidation is real but not the single purpose; the single-purpose question itself is abandoned. Critics now press two distinct objections that are often conflated. The first accepts the consumer-price frame and argues the measurements were done badly, since quality degradation, data extraction, and forgone innovation are price-like harms the cases ignored. The second rejects the frame itself, holding that concentrated private power injures suppliers, workers, and the openness of markets in ways no consumer metric captures. Defenders answer that the first critique is absorbable and the second is a mandate for judges to weigh incommensurable values without a scale. The live dispute is therefore not whether the old standard missed harms. It is whether the remedy is better measurement inside the frame or replacement of the frame, at the cost of the predictability that made the standard attractive.\\", \\"credited_answers\\": [\\"Everyone now grants the consumer-price standard missed real harms; the fight is between fixing its measurements within the frame and replacing the frame to reach power-based harms, at the price of the predictability that justified it.\\"], \\"explanation\\": \\"The big ideas your summary has to carry: (1) old anchor: consumer prices, prized for predictability · (2) critique one: right frame, bad measurement (quality, data, innovation) · (3) critique two: wrong frame, power harms beyond consumers · (4) defenders\'\' reply and the real dispute: repair vs replace, with predictability as the stake.
